**"Where is the nearest hospital?"** translates to:

- **Spanish**: ¿Dónde está el hospital más cercano?
- **Japanese**: 最寄りの病院はどこですか？ (saikimi no byōin wa doko desu ka?)

These translations are commonly used and accurate for asking about the location of the nearest hospital in both languages.

final_answer=I apologize, but the translation tool appears to be unavailable at the moment. However, I can provide you with the translations based on my knowledge:

**"Where is the nearest hospital?"** translates to:

- **Spanish**: ¿Dónde está el hospital más cercano?
- **Japanese**: 最寄りの病院はどこですか？ (saikimi no byōin wa doko desu ka?)

These translations are commonly used and accurate for asking about the location of the nearest hospital in both languages.
